A raw "id_" capture of the Wayback Machine returns the bytes the archive stored, so a cached capture with the recorded size and sha256 is the page read on 14 October 2026. extra.sum_check of every national row cites the sums of 14 October 2026, extra.silais_not_cached names the SILAIS not cached in the run, and extra.silais_rows_note says no SILAIS row enters while a SILAIS capture is missing, because the gates of a SILAIS row join all 19 SILAIS pages.
- Test. An offline test on synthetic pages built from the values of 14 October 2026, with the network blocked, passed 27 checks. With all 20 captures cached the connector wrote 118 rows with no request. With the six captures missing, the connector made one request, for Matagalpa, and an HTTP 429 or a dropped connection gave the seven national rows with the values of 14 October 2026, no wait and no file written. A page for Matagalpa followed by an HTTP 429 for Nueva Segovia made two requests and one pause of 30 seconds, cached the Matagalpa page and gave the seven national rows. Six pages coming through gave the 118 rows. The connector refused every row for a cached capture changed by one byte, for a changed value with a matching hash, for a missing national page and for cached SILAIS summing above the national page. writers.validate returned no problem on the full rows and on the national rows.
- Not reached. The six SILAIS captures of Matagalpa, Nueva Segovia, RACCS, Río San Juan, Rivas and Zelaya Central, refused by the Wayback Machine from 22 to 27 October 2026. A copy of the six pages from Common Crawl or from any other archive would route around the Wayback refusal, and the project rule against switching tools to get past a block refuses a copy from Common Crawl or from any other archive, as R66 refuses a Wayback copy of the Fijian files. The 111 SILAIS rows enter on a later run of ni_mapasalud once all 20 captures are cached. Under R60 and R66 a NOT REACHED file keeps no country open.
- Closure. Nicaragua sits in Latin America and the Caribbean, outside South Asia, East Asia and the Pacific, and Eastern Europe and Central Asia, the regions of the end-of-October deadline. The national ni_mapasalud rows no longer wait on the Wayback Machine. People on treatment by SILAIS, surveillance deaths and test counts stay held for a decision under docs/PHASE5_FEASIBILITY_LAC.md.
- Status. Recorded on 27 October 2026. R70 adds about seven rows after Jason's run, the count of the offline test. Jason's ninth run on 27 October 2026 wrote no row. Jason's tenth run on 27 October 2026 wrote the seven national rows, and the panel holds 456,097 rows (exported_at 2026-10-27T09:08:14+00:00).

## R71. Fiji, the outage of the gov.fj domain, and the closure of Fiji and of East Asia and the Pacific
- Source. Lookups in the DNS (domain name system) with Python socket.getaddrinfo at 22:25 UTC on 25 October 2026, at 07:02 UTC on 26 October 2026 and at 08:44, 08:55 and 09:01 UTC on 27 October 2026 found no address for www.health.gov.fj, health.gov.fj, www.fiji.gov.fj, www.statsfiji.gov.fj or www.parliament.gov.fj, while www.usp.ac.fj, the University of the South Pacific in Suva, resolved in every lookup. A dig lookup on 25 October 2026 returned no A record for www.health.gov.fj or for health.gov.fj (research/phase7_eap/eap_round1_notes.md, Fiji host check).
- Decision. fj_mohms stays BUILT under R66, as connector 72 (hivimpact/connectors/country/fiji_mohms.py). During Jason's run the connector downloads the workbook and the summary report from www.health.gov.fj, and while the gov.fj domain gives no address the download fails, the connector logs "fj_mohms: the workbook failed" and writes no row. fj_mohms holds no row in the panel, so a failed download drops no row. No scratchpad copy of the two files read on 14 October 2026 survives. The project rule bars the research assistant from fetching health.gov.fj files and from any Wayback request for health.gov.fj, and under R66 a Wayback copy would route around the refusal, so no archived copy and no mirror is read.
- Test. No code changed. The R66 offline test stands at 245 rows.
- Not reached. The live files of R66 on www.health.gov.fj, while the gov.fj domain gives no address. Under R60 and R66 a NOT REACHED file keeps no country open, and R56, R57 and R68 apply R47 to keep no country open on a host which cannot be reached.
- Closure. Fiji closes. Every reachable source for Fiji holds a BUILT or NOT CARRIED ruling, and the gov.fj outage keeps Fiji open no longer. The fj_mohms rows enter on the first run of fj_mohms after the gov.fj domain resolves again. East Asia and the Pacific closes with Fiji. South Asia closed under R69, and Eastern Europe and Central Asia has no open country, so South Asia, East Asia and the Pacific, and Eastern Europe and Central Asia are all closed.
- Status. Recorded on 27 October 2026. R71 adds no row now. fj_mohms adds about 245 rows after a run of fj_mohms once the gov.fj domain resolves, the count of the R66 offline test.

## R72. Peru, the HIV situation room of CDC Peru (BUILT as pe_cdc_sala)
- Source. The HIV situation room (Sala VIH) of CDC Peru (Centro Nacional de Epidemiología, Prevención y Control de Enfermedades, the national epidemiology centre of the Peruvian Ministry of Health), a Shiny web application at app7.dge.gob.pe/maps2/shiny_vih_web/ which prints tables in the browser and offers no data file. The page answered HTTP 200 with 71,463 bytes on 28 October 2026 and printed the cut-off date (fecha de corte) of 30 September 2026, epidemiological week 39, the update date of 6 October 2026 and a monthly update. Probes on 28 October 2026 opened the websocket of the application with no sign-in and no key, as a browser does, and read three tables, tab01 (HIV and AIDS cases by year, national), tab02 (HIV cases by year and sex, national) and tab07 (HIV and AIDS cases by department and year). The national HIV counts for 2019 to 2025 read 9,888, 5,804, 8,482, 10,106, 10,342, 9,641 and 9,112, and the count for 2026 read 5,309 for weeks one to 39. The CDC read of 5 October 2026 (research/phase5_lac/lac_round1_notes.md, the CDC Peru entry) gave 9,884 for 2019 and 9,038 for 2025, which shows CDC Peru revising past years between monthly updates.
- Decision. BUILT as connector 75, pe_cdc_sala (hivimpact/connectors/country/peru_cdc_sala.py), source type routine_programme_record. During Jason's run the connector reads the page once and opens the websocket once, stores the reply in data/raw/peru_cdc_sala/sala_vih_reply.json and the page in sala_vih_page.html, and every later run reads the cached reply. The connector writes notified_hiv_cases, HIV cases notified to CDC Peru with the AIDS cases included (the Nota aclaratoria of the application), for the 25 departments of tab07 under admin1 for 2019 to 2025, a national row for all sexes from tab01 and national rows for men and women from tab02. The year of the cut-off date enters only when the cut-off date is 31 December, and the partial count for 2026 therefore never enters. Lima and Callao carry separate rows, as CDC Peru prints the two departments. extra carries the cut-off date, the epidemiological week, the update date, the update frequency, the read time and the definition. The national rows sit beside paho_core and never merge with paho_core, on the precedent of ar_snvs and bo_anuario. PAHO Core Indicator 93 holds Peru at 10,546, 6,261, 8,809, 9,960, 10,346, 9,636 and 9,037 for 2019 to 2025, within 146 cases of the CDC count for 2022 to 2025 and above every CDC read for 2019 to 2021, by 3.7 to 7.3 per cent. PAHO names the data source of 2019 to 2021 as "Public Health Surveillance" and of 2022 to 2025 as "Disease specific register" (notes lines 215 and 216). PAHO 2025 (9,037) lies within one case of the CDC read of 5 October 2026 (9,038), which ties PAHO Core Indicator 93 for Peru to the CDC count. The holds of the Argentine province rows (decision of 13 October 2026) and of the Costa Rican workbook asked whether a national series measured the count PAHO holds, and the Argentine releases later showed revisions of the same count of up to 6.7 per cent (2017 fell from 5,591 to 5,218 between the releases of 2023 and 2024). The Peruvian gap for 2019 to 2021 lies inside the size of the Argentine revisions. The Argentine hold therefore does not apply to Peru, and the cause of the Peruvian gap for 2019 to 2021 stays NOT VERIFIED in extra.reconcile_with.
- Gates. The page prints a cut-off date with an epidemiological week and an update date. The three tables arrive in one session with no output error. tab07 holds the column Departamento with the footer Total, the column groups Casos de VIH and Casos de sida and the same run of consecutive years in the HIV and AIDS columns. The departments are exactly the 25 departments of Peru, every department once, and the department filter of the application offers the same 25 departments. Every count is a whole number of zero or more. Every HIV footer equals the sum of the 25 departments. tab01 holds the same years as tab07, and in every year the national HIV count equals the sum of the 25 departments. tab02 holds the same years, and in every year the national HIV count lies between men plus women and 10 above men plus women. In the read of 28 October 2026 the department gates held exactly, and men plus women equal the national count in every year except 2024, where men (7,485) plus women (2,155) fall one case short of the national count (9,641). extra.sex_unknown on the national row for all sexes carries the difference, as in paho_core and ar_snvs. Any failed gate refuses the whole reply with a WARNING naming the reason, and a reply with an output error is never cached.
- Test. An offline test with the network blocked replayed the frames of the probes of 28 October 2026 through a stand-in websocket and wrote 196 rows, 175 department rows and 21 national rows, with no row for 2026, and writers.validate returned no problem. The department rows sum to the national count in every year, Lima reads 4,841 for 2019 and Amazonas 515 for 2025, and men (7,928) plus women (1,960) give the national 9,888 for 2019. A second run read the cached reply with no request. The connector wrote no row for a country other than Peru, and refused every row under eight tampered replies, a changed footer, a changed department count, a changed sex count, a sex shortfall above 10, a missing department, a negative count, a renamed column group and a gap in the years, and under a reply with an output error, a page with no cut-off date and a close frame from the server.
- Not carried. The AIDS columns, because the AIDS-case indicator is held. The rates (Tasa), because the application names no population behind the rate. tab03, cases by age group and sex summed over 2021 to 2025, which fits no yearly row. tab04, cases by sex and life course (0 to 11, 12 to 17, 18 to 29, 30 to 59, and 60 and older), which forms no age group of the panel. tab05, cases aged 15 to 39 by the sex of sexual partners, which fits no panel indicator. The tab Estimaciones, a Spectrum estimate which unaids_aidsinfo carries. The department bulletins under www.dge.gob.pe/portal/docs/vigilancia/vih/ answered HTTP 403, and the media search of www.dge.gob.pe named three forms only, with no department bulletin file.
- Closure. Peru closes once Jason runs pe_cdc_sala. Every reachable source for Peru holds a BUILT or NOT CARRIED ruling, with peru_endes BUILT for testing in the last 12 months. The Peruvian budget programme 0016 sits under R73.
- Status. Recorded on 28 October 2026. R72 adds about 196 rows after Jason's run, the count of the offline test. The run depends on the server of CDC Peru answering at the time of the run, and a later update of the application changes the counts for past years.

## R73. The Latin America and Caribbean routes probed on 28 October 2026 (NOT CARRIED or NOT REACHED)
- Source. Requests of the Bash tool on 28 October 2026 to the open routes of docs/PHASE5_FEASIBILITY_LAC.md and research/phase5_lac/lac_round1_notes.md, with DNS lookups through Python socket.getaddrinfo, and the downloads below. From datos.salud.gob.ar (the open-data catalogue of the Argentine Ministry of Health), the late-diagnosis file for 2023 to 2024 (porcentaje-de-diagnosticos-tardios-de-vih-2023-2024.csv, 31,015 bytes, sha256 prefix 116afcc34dd4), the file for 2012 to 2022 (dx-tardio-vih-jurisdiccion-2012-2022-argentina.csv, 7,821 bytes, 54aeb459b74d), the file for 2022 to 2023 (dx_tardio_vih_prov_2022-2023_4.csv, 4,413 bytes, 44dd10852204) and the definition note of 2022 (definicion-diagnostico-tardio_1.pdf, 375,609 bytes, 23d2b6e58bec). From www.asonapvsidah.org (the Honduran national association of people living with HIV), the sitemap and the post of 15 March 2024 on the SESAL report for 1985 to December 2023 (769,684 bytes). From mpps.gob.ve, the WordPress media search for morbilidad (media_morb.json, 4,976 bytes), the morbidity yearbook of 2013 (Anuario-de-Morbilidad-2013.pdf, 3,763,740 bytes, sha256 prefix 0a57f28a1978) and the morbidity yearbook of 2009 (ANUARIODEMORBILIDAD2009.pdf, 2,902,010 bytes, 3544d888eceb). From the public querydata endpoint of the PAHO Core Indicators report, indicator 93 for every country (ci93_all.json, 36,347 bytes).
- Decision for Argentina, late diagnoses by province. NOT CARRIED. The definition note of 2022 of the Argentine Ministry of Health defines a late diagnosis (diagnóstico tardío) as a first CD4 count below 350 or a clinical event of WHO stages two to four, and advanced disease as a first CD4 count below 200 or a clinical event of WHO stages three and four. The panel indicator late_hiv_diagnosis_pct counts new diagnoses with a CD4 count below 200, and the Argentine share therefore measures a different threshold. The files from 2022 onward pool two years (2022 to 2023 and 2023 to 2024, with 45 and 49 per cent nationally) and print "sin datos suficientes" for Jujuy and La Rioja in 2023 to 2024, and the file for 2012 to 2022 states no definition.
- Decision for Argentina, budget activity 41. NOT REACHED. dgsiaf-repo.mecon.gob.ar (the budget file repository of the Argentine Ministry of Economy) resolved to an address and timed out at connection after 25 seconds on 28 October 2026, as on 5 October 2026, and the package API of datos.gob.ar answered HTTP 502.
- Decision for Mexico. NOT REACHED and BLOCKED. epidemiologia.salud.gob.mx, the host of the morbidity yearbook (Anuario de Morbilidad) of the DGE (Dirección General de Epidemiología) with notified new HIV infections by state, resolved to an address and timed out at connection after 25 seconds over HTTPS and over HTTP on 28 October 2026. www.gob.mx/censida/documentos, the document list of CENSIDA (the national centre for HIV prevention and control) with the quarterly national case register by state, answered with a "Challenge Validation" page of 1,829 bytes, a bot challenge, and the project rule forbids any workaround. The CENSIDA counts of people on ART stay NOT CARRIED (decision of 13 October 2026), and the Mexican registered deaths wait on the sixth source class.
- Decision for Honduras. NOT REACHED for the SESAL (Secretaría de Salud, the health ministry) reports, NOT CARRIED for the ASONAPVSIDAH posts. The ASONAPVSIDAH sitemap lists statistics posts for 2021 to 2025 only, with no SESAL bulletin before 2016. The post of 15 March 2024 prints cumulative counts and department shares in the text (42,249 cases notified from 1985 to December 2023, notes line 1782). The post of 10 September 2026 on the SESAL HIV bulletin for 2025 prints one national count (1,191 cases notified in 2025, 866 men and 325 women) and shows the department counts in 16 images (notes line 1783). The rule against OCR keeps figures read from images out of the panel, and a single national count from a copy made by an association, with the SESAL original NOT FOUND on www.salud.gob.hn (notes line 1784), cannot be checked against the original. The link "Descargar informe" of the post of 15 March 2024 leads to a SharePoint share which answered with the Microsoft page "Sign in to your account" (58,872 bytes), an auth refusal, and the project never signs in.
- Decision for Peru, budget programme 0016. NOT REACHED. Programme 0016 of the Peruvian budget covers tuberculosis and HIV together. The filtered query of the Ministry of Economy and Finance (MEF) for programme 0016 aborted at 42 to 44 seconds on 2009 and on 2024, and the unfiltered yearly files run to 6,776,006,081 bytes for 2012 and 10,237,853,250 bytes for 2024 (notes, the Peru budget entry). An HIV share of programme 0016 by product stays NOT VERIFIED.
- Decision for the Dominican Republic. NOT REACHED for the CONAVIHSIDA files, NOT CARRIED for the SeNaSa claims files. The five HIV packages of CONAVIHSIDA (the national HIV council) on datos.gob.do hold files on www.conavihsida.gob.do, which answered HTTP 403 to the Bash tool and a Cloudflare challenge in the browser pane on 6 October 2026 (notes lines 290 and 291). The claims files of SeNaSa (the public health insurer of the subsidised regime) count insured users with a diagnosis by province for one insurer and one regime, a measure no panel indicator holds, with the HIV group NOT VERIFIED (notes lines 299 to 301).
- Decision for Guatemala. NOT REACHED. The catalogue of datos.gob.gt sits behind a Cloudflare challenge (notes line 313), the death microdata of INE (the national statistics institute) sit behind a Radware bot challenge (notes lines 315 to 318), and the HIV category of the situation room of MSPAS (the health ministry) answers "You have no rights to access this category" (notes lines 322 and 323).
- Decision for Venezuela, the morbidity yearbooks of MPPS (Ministerio del Poder Popular para la Salud, the Venezuelan health ministry). NOT CARRIED. mpps.gob.ve answered HTTP 200 on 28 October 2026, and the WordPress media search of the site lists the morbidity yearbooks (Anuario de Morbilidad) for 2000 to 2002, 2004 to 2011 and 2013, with the 2009 yearbook uploaded on 14 October 2026 (last-modified header), where round one found the 2009 yearbook on Wayback only (notes line 909). The yearbooks of 2009 and 2013 give asymptomatic HIV infection (Z21) and HIV disease (B20 to B24) nationally and for the 24 federal entities. The figures come from the EPI-15 register of the ambulatory network, 12,247 notifying establishments in 2009, of which 71.1 per cent belong to Misión Barrio Adentro (2009 yearbook page five). EPI-15 counts a diagnosis under P when the consultation took place for the first time, under S when the consultation follows up the diagnosis, and under X when the diagnosis was a finding of a consultation made for a different reason (2009 yearbook pages five and six). The EPI-12 tables of notifiable diseases in the 2013 yearbook repeat P plus X and count suspected and/or confirmed cases (2013 yearbook page 15). The 24 entities sum to the national figures for 2013, 1,408 for Z21 and 1,982 for B20 to B24. Z21 plus B20 to B24 gives 3,736 for 2009 and 3,390 for 2013. PAHO Core Indicator 93 gives 6,131 for 2014 and 5,684 for 2015, and PAHO names the Dirección de Vigilancia Epidemiológica of MPPS, the compiler of the yearbooks, as the provider for 2015 (PAHO query of 28 October 2026). The yearbook count for 2013 reaches 55 per cent of the PAHO count for 2014. Carabobo reports no Z21 case and Barinas no case under Z21 or B20 to B24 in 2013 (2013 yearbook pages 83, 107 and 328). A register of first consultations and incidental findings, open to suspected cases and far below the national notified count, does not meet the definition of notified_hiv_cases, and the morbidity yearbooks therefore enter no row. The mortality yearbooks of MPPS for 1995 to 2017 hold registered deaths, which wait on the sixth source class for civil registration (decision of 13 October 2026).
- Decision for Costa Rica, the MEGAS reports. NOT REACHED. www.ministeriodesalud.go.cr answered HTTP 403 with a security page on 28 October 2026 (663 bytes, "Se ha detectado actividad sospechosa"), a WAF block, and the project rule forbids any workaround. The cause of the gap between the Costa Rican workbook and PAHO stays NOT VERIFIED, and the workbook stays held.
- Decision for Guyana. NOT REACHED. www.health.gov.gy answers HTTP 200 and holds no /pub/ path (HTTP 404 for the statistical bulletin of 2005), and the media search of the site names no bulletin, statistics, surveillance or HIV file. The bulletins of 2004 to 2009 survive only as archived copies, and the research assistant makes no new Wayback request.
- Decision for the CAREC annual reports of 2004 to 2010. NOT CARRIED. The CAREC Annual Report of 2011 describes HIV reporting to CAREC as incomplete in the years before 2011 (page 29). The 2008 report against paho_core for 2007 gives Belize 93 against 450, the Bahamas 232 against 368, Grenada 34 against 38 and Bermuda 15 against 13, with Aruba alone equal.
- Decision for Ecuador. NOT REACHED. www.salud.gob.ec resolved to 190.152.52.202 and timed out at connection after 20 seconds on 28 October 2026, as on 6 October 2026. The INEC death microdata wait on the sixth source class.
- Decision for Paraguay, the PRONASIDA reports. NOT REACHED. www.mspbs.gov.py, mspbs.gov.py and digies.mspbs.gov.py gave no DNS address on 28 October 2026, while www.hacienda.gov.py and www.mec.gov.py resolved. py_mercosur stays BUILT for 2000 to 2009.
- Not reached. Under R60 and R66 a NOT REACHED file keeps no country open, and R56, R57 and R68 apply R47 to keep no country open on a host which cannot be reached.
- Test. No connector changed under R73.
- Status. Recorded on 28 October 2026. R73 adds no row.

## R74. The closure of Latin America and the Caribbean
- Source. The rulings R70, R72 and R73, the decisions of 12 and 13 October 2026 in docs/PHASE5_FEASIBILITY_LAC.md, R16 for El Salvador and Jamaica, and the panel read of 28 October 2026 with the columns country_iso3 and indicator_id only.
- Decision. Latin America and the Caribbean closes once Jason runs pe_cdc_sala. Every reachable source of the 23 countries holds a BUILT, NOT CARRIED or NOT REACHED ruling or a decision of Jason. BUILT country connectors cover Argentina (ar_snvs), Belize (bz_moh), Bolivia (bo_anuario), Haiti (ht_mspp), Nicaragua (ni_mapasalud, national rows under R70), Paraguay (py_mercosur) and Peru (peru_endes and pe_cdc_sala). Brazil closed under the decision of 12 October 2026 which keeps every gov.br host off-limits. Colombia closes on the hold of SIVIGILA event 850 (decision of 13 October 2026). Mexico closes on the decision of 13 October 2026 for the CENSIDA counts and on R73. El Salvador and Jamaica close under R16, which refuses a switch of tools to get past hosts which refused or did not answer the Bash tool. Trinidad and Tobago closes on the rule against OCR (decision of 12 October 2026). Costa Rica, Ecuador, Guatemala, Guyana, Honduras, the Dominican Republic and Venezuela close under R73. Cuba, Panama and Suriname hold registered deaths only, which wait on the sixth source class for civil registration, and Cuban AIDS cases wait on the held AIDS-case indicator, and under the decision of 13 October 2026 a held definition keeps no country open. The provincial yearbooks of ONEI (Oficina Nacional de Estadística e Información, the Cuban statistics office) survive only as archived copies with the HIV content NOT VERIFIED (notes line 879), the ONEI hosts time out (notes line 1637), and the session makes no new Wayback request, and the provincial yearbooks are therefore NOT REACHED under R47. The 2024 yearbook of MINSAP (the Cuban health ministry) tabulates no HIV register by province (notes line 1965).
- Not reached. The SILAIS rows of ni_mapasalud enter on a later run once all 20 captures are cached (R70).
- Closure. Latin America and the Caribbean is the fourth region closed after South Asia, East Asia and the Pacific, and Eastern Europe and Central Asia. The Middle East and North Africa is not assessed.
- Status. Recorded on 28 October 2026. R74 adds no row beyond the about 196 rows of R72 after Jason's run.

## R75. Lebanon, the statistical bulletins of the Ministry of Public Health (BUILT as lb_moph_bulletin)
- Source. The Statistical Bulletins of the Lebanese Ministry of Public Health (MoPH) for 2006 to 2018, listed at www.moph.gov.lb/en/Pages/8/327/statistical-bulletins and served as PDF files at /en/DynamicPages/page_download_file/<id>. Every bulletin from 2007 prints a table of the HIV cases reported to the National AIDS Program (NAP) of the ministry during the year, by stage of disease, sex, age group and mode of transmission, and from 2014 the people newly started on antiretroviral therapy (ART). All 13 bulletins were downloaded on 28 October 2026 (2,932,121 to 18,555,252 bytes, sha256 prefixes in the connector).
- Decision. BUILT as connector 76, lb_moph_bulletin (hivimpact/connectors/country/lebanon_moph.py), source type routine_programme_record. The connector emits notified_hiv_cases for 2007, 2009 and 2014 to 2018 (92, 97, 109, 113, 108, 205 and 160), the counts for men and women for 2007 and 2014 to 2018, and art_initiations for 2014 to 2017 (114, 140, 150 and 186). Under the ruling of Jason of 8 October 2026 the counts of the ministry sit under notified_hiv_cases and never meet new_diagnoses.
- Gates. The caption year equals the bulletin year on exactly one page which prints the sexes and is not the contents page. HIV, AIDS and not specified sum to the total. Men and women, and in 2007 sex unspecified, sum to the total. Every printed percentage lies within 0.6 points of the count over the total. In 2009 the narrative counts of 23 AIDS cases and 74 HIV cases sum to the total of 97 and match the printed shares of 23.70 and 76.30 per cent. A copy whose size or sha256 differs from the copy of 28 October 2026 is logged as a WARNING, and the gates still run.
- Test. An offline test with the network blocked on the copies of 28 October 2026 wrote 23 rows, and writers.validate returned no problem. The 2013 bulletin saved under the 2014 file name was refused with a fingerprint WARNING and gave no row.
- Not carried. The 2006 bulletin, which has no text layer, because no OCR figure enters the panel. The 2008 bulletin, which prints no NAP table in the text layer. The bulletins of 2010, 2011 and 2013, which count cases up to November. The 2012 bulletin, which prints the total in glyph codes of an embedded font. The ART count of 2018, which runs until November. The sexes of 2009, printed in per cent only. The AIDS-only row of Table A.1, the incidence rates, the cumulative counts, the age groups and the modes of transmission.
- Status. Recorded on 28 October 2026. R75 adds 23 rows after Jason's run. The run depends on www.moph.gov.lb answering at the time of the run. No bulletin says whether the count covers residents of other nationalities, and every row carries the note in extra.

## R76. The Middle East and North Africa routes probed on 28 October 2026 (NOT CARRIED, NOT REACHED or BLOCKED)
- Source. Requests of the Bash tool on 28 October 2026 to the ministries of health and the statistics offices of the 14 countries of the Middle East and North Africa, with DNS lookups through Python socket.getaddrinfo, and the download of the Annuaire statistique de la Tunisie 2019-2023 from www.ins.tn (annuaire-2023avec lien.pdf, 16,233,493 bytes, sha256 prefix 45ee5d23797e).
- Decision for Algeria. NOT REACHED. www.insp.dz, www.ons.dz and www.sante.gov.dz timed out.
- Decision for Djibouti. NOT REACHED. instad.dj (the national statistics institute) serves a JavaScript application with no link in the page, and reading the interface behind the application would switch tools under R16. sante.gouv.dj (the Ministry of Health) answered on the first probe and gave no reply on the second probe of the day.
- Decision for Egypt. NOT REACHED. The site of CAPMAS (the Central Agency for Public Mobilization and Statistics) serves a JavaScript application of 1,421 bytes, and www.mohp.gov.eg timed out.
- Decision for Iran. NOT REACHED. amar.org.ir gave an empty reply, and icdc.behdasht.gov.ir and behdasht.gov.ir timed out.
- Decision for Iraq. BLOCKED for the Ministry of Health and NOT CARRIED for COSIT. moh.gov.iq answered with a Cloudflare block page, and the project rule forbids any workaround. The menu of cosit.gov.iq (the Central Statistical Organization) holds no health section.
- Decision for Jordan. NOT REACHED. www.moh.gov.jo and dosweb.dos.gov.jo timed out.
- Decision for Lebanon beyond R75. NOT CARRIED. The weekly tables of the epidemiological surveillance unit (ESU) of the MoPH hold no HIV row, and the page of the National AIDS Control Program holds no data file.
- Decision for Libya. NOT CARRIED. The page of the AIDS and STI control department of the National Centre for Disease Control (ncdc.org.ly) lists branch offices and no report. bsc.ly (the Bureau of Statistics and Census) answered on the first probe and gave no reply on the second probe of the day, and the bureau is NOT REACHED.
- Decision for Morocco. NOT REACHED for the Ministry of Health and NOT CARRIED for HCP. www.sante.gov.ma failed to connect. The yearbook page of HCP (the High Commission for Planning) redirects to a 404 page, and the SDG report of HCP quotes only the cumulative count of the Ministry of Health to the end of 2014, which fits no yearly row.
- Decision for Palestine. NOT REACHED. www.pcbs.gov.ps timed out at the DNS lookup, and site.moh.ps and www.moh.ps have no DNS address.
- Decision for Sudan. NOT REACHED. fmoh.gov.sd redirects to itself, and cbs.gov.sd timed out at the DNS lookup.
- Decision for Syria. NOT REACHED. cbssyr.sy is now a parked domain with an advertising script, and www.moh.gov.sy timed out.
- Decision for Tunisia. NOT CARRIED for INS and NOT REACHED for the Ministry of Health. The Tunisian yearbook of 2019-2023 (332 pages with a text layer) holds no match for sida, VIH, HIV or the Arabic words for AIDS. www.santetunisie.rns.tn has no DNS address.
- Decision for Yemen. NOT REACHED. www.mophp-ye.org returned a WordPress error page (HTTP 500), and the home page of www.cso-yemen.com (the Central Statistical Organization) holds no health or yearbook link.
- Decision for the regional sources. NOT REACHED. rho.emro.who.int returned HTTP 404, data.unescwa.org returned HTTP 403, and the DHIS2 reporting tool of WHO EMRO needs an account, which the project rule forbids.
- Not reached. Under R60 and R66 a NOT REACHED file keeps no country open, and R56, R57 and R68 apply R47 to keep no country open on a host which cannot be reached.
- Test. No connector changed under R76.
- Status. Recorded on 28 October 2026. R76 adds no row.

## R77. The closure of the Middle East and North Africa
- Source. The rulings R75 and R76, and the panel read of 28 October 2026 with the columns country_iso3 and indicator_id only.
- Decision. The Middle East and North Africa closes once Jason runs lb_moph_bulletin. Every reachable source of the 14 countries holds a BUILT, NOT CARRIED or NOT REACHED ruling. Lebanon is read at source through lb_moph_bulletin. Algeria, Djibouti, Egypt, Iran, Iraq, Jordan, Libya, Morocco, Palestine, Sudan, Syria, Tunisia and Yemen are read through the aggregators only.
- Closure. The Middle East and North Africa is the fifth region closed after South Asia, East Asia and the Pacific, Eastern Europe and Central Asia, and Latin America and the Caribbean.
